1A

The Gazette of the Democratic Socialist Republic of Sri Lanka

EXTRAORDINARY

(Published by Authority)

PART IV (B) – LOCAL GOVERNMENT

Local Government Notifications

No. 2433/37 – SATURDAY, APRIL 26, 2025

PRADESHIYA SABHA - EMBILIPITIYA

IT is hereby published for the public to know that it has been decided under decision No. 01 of the decision committee held on 25.02.2025 that the roads and lanes included in the schedule published in the Gazette No. 2406/31 dated 18.10.2024 and under Section 24 of Pradeshiya Sabha Act, No. 15 of 1987 declared as the roads and lanes owned by the Embilipitiya Pradeshiya Sabha since no case has been filed in a competent court on the date specified in the said notification and as the duty vested to the Pradeshiya Sabha of Sabaragamuwa province " the series of orders regarding the preparation of plans and publication of notices showing the list of roads and lanes maintained by the Pradeshiya Sabha of Sabaragamuwa Province as publication by Governor of Subaragamuwa Province in the Extraordinary Gazette No. 2316/20 dated 24th January 2023.

It is further announced that a list of road and lane included in this schedule, and every road and lane included therein shall be deem to be the roads and lanes owned by the Embilipitiya Pradeshiya Sabha under Sub-section (5) of Section 24 of Pradesiya Sabha Act, No. 15 of 1987.

R.A.S.P. Ramanayake, Secretary and the implementation officer,

carrying out the functions, Embilipitiya Pradeshiya Sabha.

At Embilipitiya Pradeshiya Sabha,18th March, 2025.

This Gazette Extraordinary can be downloaded from www.documents.gov.lk
